# Household hh_s19 — 32 days, Monday to Thursday

Seed-generated household. Times are clock times; ids are the receptacle and object ids used in events.jsonl. A line indented under an activity says where an object went when the activity ended and why. WHIM marks a placement that landed somewhere other than where the decision was heading. Text in [brackets] names the hidden cause the decision is attributed to.

## Household

Type: flatmates. Rooms: balcony, bathroom, bedroom_1, bedroom_2, entry, kitchen, living, office, storage.

Residents:

## Day 10 — Thursday

**Active causes**

- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_1:d10`: Marco is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (16:09–16:39).

## Day 14 — Monday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_2:d14`: Omar hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (15:07–16:58).
- `ep:sore_back:resident_1:d14`: Marco has a sore back today and sits in the armchair instead of the couch, so things land on the side table (06:30–24:00).
- `sick_day:resident_1` (Marco): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `sick_day:resident_2` (Omar): Off sick, resting on the couch all day. no work or trips out; laptop, mug, medication, book and blanket all migrate to the couch and coffee table.
- `low_energy:resident_1`: Marco has low energy (0.00): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.
- `low_energy:resident_2`: Omar has low energy (0.12): leaves things where they were used instead of putting them back.

**Active causes**

- `ep:on_a_roll:resident_2:d28`: Omar is on a roll with work: sessions run long, breaks are short, the mug stays on the desk (13:25–16:37).
